    border-radius:999px;
    background:#e5e7eb;
    margin:18px 0 0;
}
.cbl-ai-seq-bar{
    width:0%;
    height:100%;
    border-radius:999px;
    background:#111827;
    transition:width .35s ease;
}
.cbl-ai-seq-bar.is-running{
    animation:cblAiSimplePulse 1.1s infinite ease-in-out;
}
@keyframes cblAiSimplePulse{
    0%{opacity:.55}
    50%{opacity:1}
    100%{opacity:.55}
}
.cbl-ai-seq-actions{
    display:flex;
    justify-content:flex-end;
    gap:8px;
    margin-top:20px;
}
.cbl-ai-seq-btn{
    border:0;
    border-radius:12px;
    padding:11px 15px;
    font-weight:950;
    cursor:pointer;
}
.cbl-ai-seq-btn.primary{
    background:#111827;
    color:#fff;
}
.cbl-ai-seq-btn.gray{
    background:#e5e7eb;
    color:#111827;
}
@media(max-width:760px){
    .cbl-ai-seq-grid{
        grid-template-columns:1fr;
    }
}
</style>

<div class="cbl-ai-seq-modal" id="cblAiSeqModal" aria-hidden="true">
    <div class="cbl-ai-seq-box">
        <div class="cbl-ai-seq-head">
            <h3>AI 글을 작성중입니다</h3>
            <span class="cbl-ai-seq-badge" id="cblAiSeqBadge">0/0</span>
        </div>

        <div class="cbl-ai-seq-status" id="cblAiSeqStatus">
            준비중입니다...
            <span class="cbl-ai-seq-sub">선택한 글을 순차적으로 생성합니다.</span>
        </div>

        <div class="cbl-ai-seq-progress">
            <div class="cbl-ai-seq-bar" id="cblAiSeqBar"></div>
        </div>

        <div class="cbl-ai-seq-actions">
            <button type="button" class="cbl-ai-seq-btn gray" id="cblAiSeqCancelBtn">중단</button>
            <button type="button" class="cbl-ai-seq-btn primary" id="cblAiSeqReloadBtn" style="display:none;">새로고침</button>
        </div>
    </div>
</div>

<script>
(function(){
    if(window.__CBL_AI_SEQ_SIMPLE__) return;
    window.__CBL_AI_SEQ_SIMPLE__ = true;

    const __cblAiSeqPath = window.location.pathname || "";

    // AI 순차 생성 스크립트는 자동글 생성 화면 전용.
    // 글 작성/수정 화면에서는 수정저장 submit을 절대 가로채지 않는다.
    if (/\/post\/\d+\/edit\/?$/.test(__cblAiSeqPath) || __cblAiSeqPath.includes("/post/add")) {
        return;
    }

    const $ = (s, r=document) => r.querySelector(s);
    const $$ = (s, r=document) => Array.from(r.querySelectorAll(s));

    let activeController = null;
    let cancelRequested = false;
    let running = false;

    function norm(v){
        return String(v || "").replace(/\s+/g," ").trim().toLowerCase();
    }

    function labelText(el){
        let parts = [];

        parts.push(el.name || "");
        parts.push(el.id || "");
        parts.push(el.className || "");
        parts.push(el.placeholder || "");
        parts.push(el.getAttribute("aria-label") || "");
        parts.push(el.getAttribute("title") || "");

        if(el.id){
            const label = document.querySelector(`label[for="${CSS.escape(el.id)}"]`);
            if(label) parts.push(label.innerText || label.textContent || "");
        }

        const label = el.closest("label");
        if(label) parts.push(label.innerText || label.textContent || "");

        const parent = el.parentElement;
        if(parent) parts.push(parent.innerText || parent.textContent || "");

        return norm(parts.join(" "));
    }

    function isAiForm(form){
        const path = window.location.pathname || "";

        if (/\/post\/\d+\/edit\/?$/.test(path) || path.includes("/post/add")) {
            return false;
        }

        const action = norm(form.getAttribute("action") || form.action || "");
        const text = norm(form.innerText || form.textContent || "");
        const html = norm(form.outerHTML || "");

        const hasCoreFields =
            form.querySelector('[name="keywords"]') &&
            form.querySelector('[name="category"]') &&
            form.querySelector('[name="writing_style"]');

        if(!hasCoreFields) return false;

        if(action.includes("/ai-post/generate")) return true;
        if(action.includes("ai-post/generate")) return true;
        if(html.includes("ai_post_generate")) return true;
        if(html.includes("ai-post/generate")) return true;
        if(form.id === "ai-post-form") return true;
        if(form.querySelector("#ai-keywords") || form.querySelector("#ai-selected-keywords")) return true;

        return (
            text.includes("자동글") ||
            text.includes("ai 글") ||
            text.includes("주요 이슈 키워드")
        );
    }

    function getAiForms(){
        const forms = $$("form").filter(isAiForm);

        forms.forEach(form => {
            $$("[data-cbl-ai-seq-options], .cbl-ai-seq-options", form)
                .slice(1)
                .forEach(el => el.remove());
        });

        const visible = forms.filter(form => {
            return !!(form.offsetWidth || form.offsetHeight || form.getClientRects().length);
        });

        return visible.length ? [visible[0]] : forms.slice(0, 1);
    }

    function langKind(el){
        const t = labelText(el);

        if(
            t.includes("영어") ||
            t.includes("english") ||
            t.includes("_en") ||
            t.includes(" en ")
        ){
            return "en";
        }

        if(
            t.includes("한글") ||
            t.includes("한국어") ||
            t.includes("korean") ||
            t.includes("_ko") ||
            t.includes(" ko ")
        ){
            return "ko";
        }

        return "";
    }

    function getSelectedLanguages(form){
        const boxes = $$('input[type="checkbox"]', form).filter(el => {
            if(el.closest("[data-cbl-ai-seq-options]")) return false;
            return !!langKind(el);
        });

        const koBoxes = boxes.filter(el => langKind(el) === "ko");
        const enBoxes = boxes.filter(el => langKind(el) === "en");

        const koChecked = koBoxes.length ? koBoxes.some(el => el.checked) : true;
        const enChecked = enBoxes.length ? enBoxes.some(el => el.checked) : false;

        const langs = [];

        if(koChecked) langs.push("ko");
        if(enChecked) langs.push("en");

        if(!langs.length) langs.push("ko");

        return langs;
    }

    function getPostCount(form){
        const candidates = [];

        candidates.push(...$$("input, select", form));
        candidates.push(...$$("input, select", document));

        for(const el of candidates){
            const t = labelText(el);

            if(t.includes("image_count") || t.includes("이미지 개수")) continue;
            if(t.includes("조회수") || t.includes("view")) continue;
            if(t.includes("댓글") || t.includes("comment")) continue;

            const looksCount =
                t.includes("count") ||
                t.includes("post_count") ||
                t.includes("generate_count") ||
                t.includes("생성 개수") ||
                t.includes("글 개수") ||
                t.includes("몇 개") ||
                t.includes("몇개");

            if(!looksCount) continue;

            const v = parseInt(String(el.value || "").trim(), 10);

            if(Number.isFinite(v) && v >= 1 && v <= 20){
                return v;
            }
        }

        return 1;
    }

    function buildJobs(form){
        const count = getPostCount(form);
        const langs = getSelectedLanguages(form);
        const jobs = [];

        for(let i = 0; i < count; i++){
            for(const lang of langs){
                jobs.push({lang});
            }
        }

        return jobs;
    }

    function ensurePanel(form){
        if(!form || form.querySelector("[data-cbl-ai-seq-options]")) return;

        const currentPath = window.location.pathname || "";

        // 글 작성/수정 화면에는 AI 순차 생성 옵션 박스를 붙이지 않는다.
        // 이 옵션은 자동글 생성 화면 전용이다.
        if (/\/post\/\d+\/edit\/?$/.test(currentPath) || currentPath.includes("/post/add")) {
            return;
        }

        const panel = document.createElement("div");
        panel.className = "cbl-ai-seq-options";
        panel.setAttribute("data-cbl-ai-seq-options","1");
        panel.innerHTML = `
            <div class="cbl-ai-seq-options-title">AI 생성 옵션</div>
            <div class="cbl-ai-seq-grid">
                <label class="cbl-ai-seq-check">
                    <input type="checkbox" data-cbl-opt="content_image" checked>
                    <span>본문 이미지 자동 생성</span>
                </label>
                <label class="cbl-ai-seq-check">
                    <input type="checkbox" data-cbl-opt="thumbnail" checked>
                    <span>자동 썸네일 생성</span>
                </label>
                <label class="cbl-ai-seq-check">
                    <input type="checkbox" data-cbl-opt="tags" checked>
                    <span>자동 태그 생성</span>
                </label>
                <label class="cbl-ai-seq-check">
                    <input type="checkbox" data-cbl-opt="draft" checked>
                    <span>비공개 초안 저장</span>
                </label>
            </div>
            <div class="cbl-ai-seq-help">
                선택한 언어와 글 개수에 맞춰 1개씩 순차 생성합니다.
            </div>
        `;

        const submit = form.querySelector('button[type="submit"], input[type="submit"], button:not([type])');

        if(submit && submit.parentElement){
            submit.parentElement.insertAdjacentElement("beforebegin", panel);
        }else{
            form.appendChild(panel);
        }
    }

    function modalEls(){
        return {
            modal: $("#cblAiSeqModal"),
            badge: $("#cblAiSeqBadge"),
            status: $("#cblAiSeqStatus"),
            bar: $("#cblAiSeqBar"),
            cancelBtn: $("#cblAiSeqCancelBtn"),
            reloadBtn: $("#cblAiSeqReloadBtn"),
        };
    }

    function openModal(total){
        const e = modalEls();

        cancelRequested = false;
        running = true;

        e.modal.classList.add("is-open");
        e.modal.setAttribute("aria-hidden","false");
        e.reloadBtn.style.display = "none";
        e.cancelBtn.style.display = "inline-block";
        e.cancelBtn.textContent = "중단";

        e.badge.textContent = `0/${total}`;
        e.status.innerHTML = `준비중입니다...<span class="cbl-ai-seq-sub">선택한 글을 순차적으로 생성합니다.</span>`;
        e.bar.style.width = "0%";
        e.bar.classList.remove("is-running");
    }

    function closeModal(){
        const e = modalEls();
        e.modal.classList.remove("is-open");
        e.modal.setAttribute("aria-hidden","true");
    }

    function setProgress(now,total,langLabel){
        const e = modalEls();
        const pct = total ? Math.round(((now - 1) / total) * 100) : 0;

        e.badge.textContent = `${now}/${total}`;
        e.status.innerHTML = `${now}/${total} 작성중입니다...<span class="cbl-ai-seq-sub">${langLabel} 글을 생성하고 있습니다.</span>`;
        e.bar.style.width = `${Math.max(2, Math.min(96, pct))}%`;
        e.bar.classList.add("is-running");
    }

    function setDone(now,total){
        const e = modalEls();
        const pct = total ? Math.round((now / total) * 100) : 100;

        e.badge.textContent = `${now}/${total}`;
        e.bar.style.width = `${pct}%`;
        e.bar.classList.remove("is-running");
    }

    function finish(total,failed){
        const e = modalEls();

        running = false;
        activeController = null;

        e.bar.classList.remove("is-running");
        e.cancelBtn.style.display = "none";
        e.reloadBtn.style.display = "inline-block";

        if(failed > 0){
            e.status.innerHTML = `일부 생성 완료<span class="cbl-ai-seq-sub">실패 ${failed}개가 있습니다. 글 목록을 확인하세요.</span>`;
        }else{
            e.status.innerHTML = `AI 글 생성 완료<span class="cbl-ai-seq-sub">총 ${total}개 생성 작업이 끝났습니다.</span>`;
            e.bar.style.width = "100%";
            e.badge.textContent = `${total}/${total}`;
        }
    }

    function checked(form,opt,def=true){
        const el = form.querySelector(`[data-cbl-opt="${opt}"]`);
        return el ? el.checked : def;
    }

    function setBool(fd,name,on){
        fd.delete(name);
        if(on) fd.append(name,"on");
    }

    function applyCommonOptions(form,fd){
        const contentImage = checked(form,"content_image",true);
        const thumbnail = checked(form,"thumbnail",true);
        const tags = checked(form,"tags",true);
        const draft = checked(form,"draft",true);

        fd.set("count","1");

        if(contentImage){
            const cur = parseInt(fd.get("image_count") || "0",10);
            if(!cur || cur < 1) fd.set("image_count","1");
        }else{
            fd.set("image_count","0");
        }

        setBool(fd,"make_thumbnail",thumbnail);
        setBool(fd,"include_tags",tags);

        setBool(fd,"make_content_image",contentImage);
        setBool(fd,"generate_content_image",contentImage);
        setBool(fd,"auto_thumbnail",thumbnail);
        setBool(fd,"auto_tags",tags);

        fd.delete("publish_immediately");

        if(!draft){
            fd.append("publish_immediately","on");
        }

        fd.set("cbl_sequential_generate","1");
    }

    function applyLanguage(form,fd,lang){
        const boxes = $$('input[type="checkbox"]', form).filter(el => {
            if(el.closest("[data-cbl-ai-seq-options]")) return false;
            return !!langKind(el);
        });

        for(const el of boxes){
            if(!el.name) continue;

            const kind = langKind(el);
            fd.delete(el.name);

            if(kind === lang){
                fd.append(el.name, el.value || "on");
            }
        }

        fd.set("cbl_seq_lang", lang);
        fd.set("target_language", lang);
        fd.set("only_language", lang);
        fd.set("cbl_only_language", "1");
    }

    async function postJob(form,job,index,total){
        const fd = new FormData(form);

        applyCommonOptions(form,fd);
        applyLanguage(form,fd,job.lang);

        fd.set("cbl_seq_index",String(index));
        fd.set("cbl_seq_total",String(total));

        activeController = new AbortController();

        const res = await fetch(form.action || window.location.href,{
            method:(form.method || "POST").toUpperCase(),
            body:fd,
            credentials:"same-origin",
            headers:{
                "X-Requested-With":"XMLHttpRequest",
                "X-CBL-Sequential-AI":"1"
            },
            signal:activeController.signal
        });

        const text = await res.text();

        if(!res.ok){
            throw new Error(`HTTP ${res.status}`);
        }

        const lower = text.toLowerCase();

        if(
            lower.includes("503 unavailable") ||
            lower.includes("request timed out") ||
            lower.includes("traceback") ||
            lower.includes("ai 다국어 글 생성 오류")
        ){
            throw new Error("AI 생성 오류");
        }

        return true;
    }

    function lockButtons(form,disabled){
        $$('button, input[type="submit"]',form).forEach(btn => {
            btn.disabled = disabled;
        });
    }

    async function runSequential(form){
        const jobs = buildJobs(form);
        const total = jobs.length || 1;

        openModal(total);
        lockButtons(form,true);

        let failed = 0;

        try{
            for(let i=0; i<jobs.length; i++){
                if(cancelRequested) break;

                const job = jobs[i];
                const now = i + 1;
                const langLabel = job.lang === "en" ? "영어" : "한글";

                setProgress(now,total,langLabel);

                try{
                    await postJob(form,job,now,total);
                }catch(err){
                    if(cancelRequested || err.name === "AbortError"){
                        break;
                    }

                    failed += 1;
                }

                setDone(now,total);
            }

            if(cancelRequested){
                const e = modalEls();

                running = false;
                activeController = null;
                e.bar.classList.remove("is-running");
                e.cancelBtn.textContent = "닫기";
                e.status.innerHTML = `생성을 중단했습니다.<span class="cbl-ai-seq-sub">이미 서버에 들어간 요청은 잠시 후 끝날 수 있습니다.</span>`;
                return;
            }

            finish(total,failed);
        }finally{
            lockButtons(form,false);
        }
    }

    function bind(){
        const forms = getAiForms();

        forms.forEach(form => {
            ensurePanel(form);

            if(form.dataset.cblSeqBound === "1") return;
            form.dataset.cblSeqBound = "1";

            form.addEventListener("submit",function(ev){
                ev.preventDefault();
                ev.stopPropagation();
                runSequential(form);
            },true);
        });

        const e = modalEls();

        if(e.cancelBtn && !e.cancelBtn.dataset.bound){
            e.cancelBtn.dataset.bound = "1";

            e.cancelBtn.addEventListener("click",function(){
                if(running){
                    cancelRequested = true;

                    if(activeController){
                        activeController.abort();
                    }

                    const els = modalEls();
                    els.bar.classList.remove("is-running");
                    els.status.innerHTML = `생성을 중단했습니다.<span class="cbl-ai-seq-sub">창을 닫아도 됩니다.</span>`;
                    els.cancelBtn.textContent = "닫기";
                    running = false;
                    return;
                }

                closeModal();
            });
        }

        if(e.reloadBtn && !e.reloadBtn.dataset.bound){
            e.reloadBtn.dataset.bound = "1";
            e.reloadBtn.addEventListener("click",function(){
                window.location.reload();
            });
        }
    }

    if(document.readyState === "loading"){
        document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded",bind);
    }else{
        bind();
    }

    const observer = new MutationObserver(bind);
    observer.observe(document.documentElement,{
        childList:true,
        subtree:true,
        attributes:true,
        attributeFilter:["class","style"]
    });
})();
</script>
<!-- CBL_AI_SEQ_SIMPLE_END -->



<script>
/* CBL_AI_GENERATE_DRAFT_RESULT_FRONT_HOTFIX_START */
(function () {
  if (window.__cblAiGenerateDraftResultFrontHotfix) return;
  window.__cblAiGenerateDraftResultFrontHotfix = true;

  const originalFetch = window.fetch;

  function isAiGenerateUrl(input) {
    try {
      const url = typeof input === "string" ? input : (input && input.url) ? input.url : "";
      return /ai[^?#]*generate|ai_post_generate|ai-post-generate/i.test(url);
    } catch (e) {
      return false;
    }
  }

  function draftOptionEnabled() {
    try {
      const inputCandidates = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll("input, textarea, select"));
      for (const el of inputCandidates) {
        const name = ((el.name || "") + " " + (el.id || "")).toLowerCase();
        const value = String(el.value || "").toLowerCase();

        if (
          name.includes("draft") ||
          name.includes("private") ||
          name.includes("is_published") ||
          name.includes("publish_immediately")
        ) {
          if (el.type === "checkbox" || el.type === "radio") {
            if (el.checked) {
              if (name.includes("draft") || name.includes("private")) return true;
              if (name.includes("publish") && ["0", "false", "off", "no", "draft", "private"].includes(value)) return true;
            }
          } else {
            if (["0", "false", "off", "no", "draft", "private", "비공개", "초안"].includes(value)) return true;
          }
        }
      }

      const textCandidates = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll("button, label, div, span"));
      const draftEl = textCandidates.find(function (el) {
        const text = (el.textContent || "").replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim();
        return text.includes("비공개") && text.includes("초안");
      });

      if (!draftEl) return false;

      const innerInput = draftEl.querySelector && draftEl.querySelector("input[type='checkbox'], input[type='radio']");
      if (innerInput) return !!innerInput.checked;

      const cls = String(draftEl.className || "").toLowerCase();
      const aria = String(draftEl.getAttribute("aria-pressed") || draftEl.getAttribute("aria-checked") || "").toLowerCase();
      const text = draftEl.textContent || "";

      if (aria === "true") return true;
      if (cls.includes("active") || cls.includes("selected") || cls.includes("checked") || cls.includes("on")) return true;
      if (text.includes("✓") || text.includes("✔")) return true;

      return false;
    } catch (e) {
      return false;
    }
  }

  function applyDraftFieldsToBody(body) {
    const draftOn = draftOptionEnabled();
    if (!draftOn) return body;

    try {
      if (body instanceof FormData) {
        body.set("save_as_draft", "1");
        body.set("draft", "1");
        body.set("is_draft", "1");
        body.set("publish_immediately", "0");
        body.set("is_published", "0");
        return body;
      }

      if (body instanceof URLSearchParams) {
        body.set("save_as_draft", "1");
        body.set("draft", "1");
        body.set("is_draft", "1");
        body.set("publish_immediately", "0");
        body.set("is_published", "0");
        return body;
      }

      if (typeof body === "string") {
        const trimmed = body.trim();

        if (trimmed.startsWith("{") && trimmed.endsWith("}")) {
          const data = JSON.parse(trimmed);
          data.save_as_draft = true;
          data.draft = true;
          data.is_draft = true;
          data.publish_immediately = false;
          data.is_published = false;
          return JSON.stringify(data);
        }

        const params = new URLSearchParams(body);
        if (Array.from(params.keys()).length > 0) {
          params.set("save_as_draft", "1");
          params.set("draft", "1");
          params.set("is_draft", "1");
          params.set("publish_immediately", "0");
          params.set("is_published", "0");
          return params.toString();
        }
      }
    } catch (e) {
      return body;
    }

    return body;
  }

  function normalizeAiGenerateJson(data) {
    try {
      if (!data || typeof data !== "object") return data;

      let createdCount = Number(data.created_count || data.success_count || 0);

      if (!createdCount) {
        const keys = ["created_posts", "posts", "created", "created_items", "results", "items"];
        for (const key of keys) {
          if (Array.isArray(data[key])) {
            const count = data[key].filter(function (item) {
              if (!item || typeof item !== "object") return true;
              const status = String(item.status || item.result || item.state || item.message || "").toLowerCase();
              return item.post_id || item.id || item.url || item.detail_url || item.edit_url ||
                     status.includes("성공") || status.includes("완료") ||
                     ["success", "ok", "created"].includes(status);
            }).length;

            if (count > 0) {
              createdCount = count;
              break;
            }
          }
        }
      }

      if (!createdCount && (data.post_id || data.id || data.url || data.detail_url || data.edit_url)) {
        createdCount = 1;
      }

      if (createdCount > 0) {
        data.success = true;
        data.ok = true;
        data.created_count = createdCount;
        data.success_count = createdCount;
        data.failed_count = 0;
        data.error_count = 0;

        if (!data.message || String(data.message).includes("실패")) {
          data.message = createdCount === 1 ? "AI 글 생성 완료" : "AI 글 " + createdCount + "개 생성 완료";
        }
      }
    } catch (e) {}

    return data;
  }

  window.fetch = function (input, init) {
    const aiGenerate = isAiGenerateUrl(input);

    if (aiGenerate && init && init.body) {
      init = Object.assign({}, init);
      init.body = applyDraftFieldsToBody(init.body);
    }

    return originalFetch.apply(this, arguments).then(function (response) {
      if (!aiGenerate) return response;

      const clone = response.clone();

      return clone.json().then(function (data) {
        const normalized = normalizeAiGenerateJson(data);
        const headers = new Headers(response.headers);
        headers.set("Content-Type", "application/json; charset=utf-8");

        return new Response(JSON.stringify(normalized), {
          status: response.status,
          statusText: response.statusText,
          headers: headers
        });
      }).catch(function () {
        return response;
      });
    });
  };
})();
/* CBL_AI_GENERATE_DRAFT_RESULT_FRONT_HOTFIX_END */
</script>

<style>

<script>
(function(){
    const CATS = [
        ["architecture", "건축"],
        ["realestate", "부동산"],
        ["finance", "금융"],
        ["tech", "테크"],
        ["life", "일상"]
    ];

    const RECO_GROUPS = [
        ["architecture", "건축"],
        ["realestate", "부동산"],
        ["finance", "금융"],
        ["tech", "테크"],
        ["life", "일상"],
        ["auto", "시간별 자동글"]
    ];

    function csrfToken(){
        const el = document.querySelector('[name=csrfmiddlewaretoken]');
        return el ? el.value : "";
    }

    function findByText(text){
        return Array.from(document.querySelectorAll("label,div,span,h1,h2,h3,h4,p,strong,button"))
            .find(el => (el.textContent || "").trim().includes(text));
    }

    function nearestFieldBlock(el){
        if(!el) return null;
        return el.closest(".form-group,.field,.cbl-form-group,.cbl-field,.mb-3,.mb-4,div") || el.parentElement;
    }

    function optionHtml(selected){
        return CATS.map(([v,t]) => `<option value="${v}" ${v===selected ? "selected" : ""}>${t}</option>`).join("");
    }

    function imageOptionHtml(selected){
        let html = "";
        for(let i=0;i<=5;i++){
            html += `<option value="${i}" ${String(i)===String(selected) ? "selected" : ""}>${i}장</option>`;
        }
        return html;
    }

    function getDefaultCategory(){
        const old = document.querySelector('select[name="category"], select#category, #id_category');
        return old && old.value ? old.value : "architecture";
    }

    function getDefaultImageCount(){
        const old = document.querySelector('select[name="image_count"], select#image_count, #id_image_count');
        return old && old.value !== undefined ? old.value : "0";
    }

    function rowTemplate(category, imageCount, keyword){
        return `
        <div class="cbl-kw-row">
            <select class="cbl-row-category" name="keyword_categories">${optionHtml(category || getDefaultCategory())}</select>
            <select class="cbl-row-image-count" name="keyword_image_counts">${imageOptionHtml(imageCount ?? getDefaultImageCount())}</select>
            <input class="cbl-row-keyword" name="keywords" value="${escapeHtml(keyword || "")}" placeholder="예: 자동화, BIM, 공사일보, 부동산 정책">
            <button type="button" class="cbl-kw-del">삭제</button>
            <button type="button" class="cbl-kw-add">추가</button>
        </div>`;
    }

    function escapeHtml(s){
        return String(s || "")
            .replaceAll("&","&amp;")
            .replaceAll("<","&lt;")
            .replaceAll(">","&gt;")
            .replaceAll('"',"&quot;");
    }

    function addRow(panel, category, imageCount, keyword){
        const body = panel.querySelector(".cbl-kw-body");
        body.insertAdjacentHTML("beforeend", rowTemplate(category, imageCount, keyword));
        const last = body.querySelector(".cbl-kw-row:last-child .cbl-row-keyword");
        if(last && !keyword) last.focus();
    }

    function buildKeywordRows(){
        const oldKeywordInput = document.querySelector('input[name="keywords"], input[name="keyword"], input[placeholder*="금리"], input[placeholder*="자동화"]');
        if(!oldKeywordInput) return null;

        const form = oldKeywordInput.closest("form");
        if(!form) return null;

        if(form.querySelector(".cbl-kw-row-panel")) return form.querySelector(".cbl-kw-row-panel");

        const keywordBlock = nearestFieldBlock(oldKeywordInput);
        const panel = document.createElement("div");
        panel.className = "cbl-kw-row-panel";
        panel.innerHTML = `
            <div class="cbl-kw-head">
                <div>자동글 카테고리</div>
                <div>본문 이미지 개수</div>
                <div>주요 이슈 키워드</div>
                <div></div>
                <div></div>
            </div>
            <div class="cbl-kw-body"></div>
        `;

        const firstKeyword = oldKeywordInput.value || "";
        panel.querySelector(".cbl-kw-body").insertAdjacentHTML(
            "beforeend",
            rowTemplate(getDefaultCategory(), getDefaultImageCount(), firstKeyword)
        );

        if(keywordBlock){
            keywordBlock.innerHTML = "";
            keywordBlock.appendChild(panel);
        }

        const catLabel = findByText("자동글 카테고리");
        const imgLabel = findByText("본문 이미지 개수");
        [catLabel, imgLabel].forEach(label => {
            const block = nearestFieldBlock(label);
            if(block && !block.querySelector(".cbl-kw-row-panel")){
                block.style.display = "none";
            }
        });

        panel.addEventListener("click", function(e){
            if(e.target.classList.contains("cbl-kw-add")){
                addRow(panel, getDefaultCategory(), getDefaultImageCount(), "");
            }
            if(e.target.classList.contains("cbl-kw-del")){
                const rows = panel.querySelectorAll(".cbl-kw-row");
                if(rows.length <= 1){
                    const input = e.target.closest(".cbl-kw-row").querySelector(".cbl-row-keyword");
                    if(input) input.value = "";
                    return;
                }
                e.target.closest(".cbl-kw-row").remove();
            }
        });

        form.addEventListener("submit", function(){
            const rows = Array.from(panel.querySelectorAll(".cbl-kw-row"));
            rows.forEach(row => {
                const kw = row.querySelector(".cbl-row-keyword");
                if(kw && !kw.value.trim()){
                    row.remove();
                }
            });

            const firstRow = panel.querySelector(".cbl-kw-row");
            if(firstRow){
                const oldCat = document.querySelector('select[name="category"], select#category, #id_category');
                const oldImg = document.querySelector('select[name="image_count"], select#image_count, #id_image_count');
                if(oldCat) oldCat.value = firstRow.querySelector(".cbl-row-category").value;
                if(oldImg) oldImg.value = firstRow.querySelector(".cbl-row-image-count").value;
            }
        }, true);

        return panel;
    }

    async function fetchKeywords(category){
        const fd = new FormData();
        fd.append("category", category === "auto" ? "architecture" : category);
        fd.append("count", "7");
        fd.append("keyword_count", "7");
        fd.append("limit", "7");

        const res = await fetch("/ai-keywords/recommend/", {
            method:"POST",
            headers: {"X-CSRFToken": csrfToken()},
            body: fd
        });

        const data = await res.json();
        let arr = [];

        if(Array.isArray(data)) arr = data;
        else if(Array.isArray(data.keywords)) arr = data.keywords;
        else if(Array.isArray(data.recommended_keywords)) arr = data.recommended_keywords;
        else if(Array.isArray(data.items)) arr = data.items;
        else if(typeof data.keywords === "string") arr = data.keywords.split(/\n|,/);

        arr = arr.map(x => {
            if(typeof x === "string") return x.trim();
            return String(x.keyword || x.title || x.name || "").trim();
        }).filter(Boolean);

        return [...new Set(arr)].slice(0,7);
    }

    function buildRecommendArea(form, panel){
        const oldBtn = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll("button,a"))
            .find(el => (el.textContent || "").includes("오늘자 키워드 추천"));
        if(!oldBtn || form.querySelector(".cbl-reco-wrap")) return;

        const wrap = document.createElement("div");
        wrap.className = "cbl-reco-wrap";
        wrap.innerHTML = `
            <div class="cbl-reco-loading">추천 키워드를 불러오는 중입니다.</div>
            <div class="cbl-reco-grid"></div>
        `;

        oldBtn.insertAdjacentElement("afterend", wrap);

        oldBtn.addEventListener("click", async function(e){
            e.preventDefault();
            e.stopImmediatePropagation();

            wrap.classList.add("is-open");
            const loading = wrap.querySelector(".cbl-reco-loading");
            const grid = wrap.querySelector(".cbl-reco-grid");
            loading.style.display = "block";
            grid.innerHTML = "";

            for(const [category, title] of RECO_GROUPS){
                const card = document.createElement("div");
                card.className = "cbl-reco-card";
                card.innerHTML = `<h4>${title}</h4><div class="cbl-reco-list"><span class="cbl-reco-loading">불러오는 중...</span></div>`;
                grid.appendChild(card);

                try{
                    const keywords = await fetchKeywords(category);
                    const list = card.querySelector(".cbl-reco-list");
                    list.innerHTML = "";

                    if(!keywords.length){
                        list.innerHTML = `<span class="cbl-reco-loading">추천 없음</span>`;
                        continue;
                    }

                    keywords.forEach(kw => {
                        const chip = document.createElement("button");
                        chip.type = "button";
                        chip.className = "cbl-reco-chip";
                        chip.textContent = kw;
                        chip.addEventListener("click", function(){
                            const realCategory = category === "auto" ? getDefaultCategory() : category;
                            addRow(panel, realCategory, getDefaultImageCount(), kw);
                        });
                        list.appendChild(chip);
                    });
                }catch(err){
                    card.querySelector(".cbl-reco-list").innerHTML = `<span class="cbl-reco-loading">불러오기 실패</span>`;
                }
            }

            loading.style.display = "none";
        }, true);
    }

    function init(){
        const panel = buildKeywordRows();
        if(!panel) return;
        const form = panel.closest("form");
        if(form) buildRecommendArea(form, panel);
    }

    document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", init);
    setTimeout(init, 500);
})();
</script>
<!-- CBL_AI_KEYWORD_ROW_UI_END -->

<script>
(function () {
    if (window.__CBL_HIDE_TOP_AI_CATEGORY_IMAGE__) return;
    window.__CBL_HIDE_TOP_AI_CATEGORY_IMAGE__ = true;

    function ready(fn) {
        if (document.readyState === "loading") {
            document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", fn);
        } else {
            fn();
        }
    }

    function hideTopCategoryImageFields() {
        const modalForm = document.getElementById("aiPostForm");
        if (!modalForm) return;

        const category = modalForm.querySelector("#ai-category");
        const imageCount = modalForm.querySelector("#ai-image-count");

        if (!category && !imageCount) return;

        const categoryRoot = category
            ? category.closest(".ai-form-group")
            : null;

        const imageRoot = imageCount
            ? imageCount.closest(".ai-form-group")
            : null;

        if (categoryRoot) {
            categoryRoot.classList.add("cbl-hide-top-ai-category-image");
        }

        if (imageRoot) {
            imageRoot.classList.add("cbl-hide-top-ai-category-image");
        }

        const topGrid = modalForm.querySelector(".ai-form-grid");

        if (topGrid) {
            const visibleChildren = Array.from(topGrid.children).filter(function (child) {
                return !child.classList.contains("cbl-hide-top-ai-category-image");
            });

            if (visibleChildren.length === 0) {
                topGrid.classList.add("cbl-hide-top-ai-category-image");
            }
        }
    }

    ready(function () {
        hideTopCategoryImageFields();
        setTimeout(hideTopCategoryImageFields, 300);
        setTimeout(hideTopCategoryImageFields, 800);
    });
})();
</script>

<script>
(function () {
    if (window.__CBL_HARD_RENDER_TOP_KEYWORD_ROW__) return;
    window.__CBL_HARD_RENDER_TOP_KEYWORD_ROW__ = true;

    function ready(fn) {
        if (document.readyState === "loading") {
            document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", fn);
        } else {
            fn();
        }
    }

    function makeCategorySelect() {
        const el = document.createElement("select");
        el.id = "ai-category";
        el.name = "category";
        el.innerHTML = `
            <option value="architecture">건축</option>
            <option value="realestate">부동산</option>
            <option value="finance">금융</option>
            <option value="tech">테크</option>
            <option value="life">일상</option>
        `;
        return el;
    }

    function makeImageSelect() {
        const el = document.createElement("select");
        el.id = "ai-image-count";
        el.name = "image_count";
        el.innerHTML = `
            <option value="0" selected>0장</option>
            <option value="1">1장</option>
            <option value="2">2장</option>
            <option value="3">3장</option>
            <option value="4">4장</option>
            <option value="5">5장</option>
        `;
        return el;
    }

    function makeKeywordInput() {
        const el = document.createElement("input");
        el.id = "ai-keywords";
        el.name = "keywords";
        el.type = "text";
        el.placeholder = "예: 자동화, BIM, 공사일보, 부동산 정책";
        return el;
    }

    function makeHidden(id, name, value) {
        const el = document.createElement("input");
        el.id = id;
        el.name = name;
        el.type = "hidden";
        el.value = value || "";
        return el;
    }

    function nearestBlock(el) {
        if (!el) return null;
        return el.closest(".ai-form-group") || el.closest(".ai-form-grid") || el.parentElement;
    }

    function findWritingGroup(form) {
        const el = form.querySelector("#ai-title-style");
        return nearestBlock(el);
    }

    function findExtraGroup(form) {
        const el = form.querySelector("#ai-extra");
        return nearestBlock(el);
    }

    function hideEmptyOldBlocks(form, panel, todayBox) {
        Array.from(form.querySelectorAll(".ai-form-grid, .ai-form-group")).forEach(function (block) {
            if (panel && panel.contains(block)) return;
            if (todayBox && todayBox.contains(block)) return;
            if (block.querySelector("#ai-title-style")) return;
            if (block.querySelector("#ai-extra")) return;
            if (block.querySelector(".ai-option-box")) return;

            const text = (block.innerText || "").replace(/\s+/g, "");

            if (
                text.includes("자동글카테고리") ||
                text.includes("생성할글개수") ||
                text.includes("본문이미지개수") ||
                text.includes("주요이슈키워드") ||
                text === ""
            ) {
                block.style.display = "none";
            }
        });
    }

    function mountTopRow() {
        const form = document.getElementById("aiPostForm");
        if (!form) return;

        const intro = form.parentElement ? form.parentElement.querySelector(".ai-post-intro") : null;
        if (intro) intro.remove();

        let category = document.getElementById("ai-category") || form.querySelector('select[name="category"]') || makeCategorySelect();
        let image = document.getElementById("ai-image-count") || form.querySelector('select[name="image_count"]') || makeImageSelect();
        let keyword = document.getElementById("ai-keywords") || form.querySelector('input[name="keywords"]') || makeKeywordInput();
        let count = document.getElementById("ai-count") || form.querySelector('input[name="count"]') || makeHidden("ai-count", "count", "1");
        let selected = document.getElementById("ai-selected-keywords") || makeHidden("ai-selected-keywords", "selected_keywords", "");

        category.id = "ai-category";
        category.name = "category";

        image.id = "ai-image-count";
        image.name = "image_count";

        keyword.id = "ai-keywords";
        keyword.name = "keywords";
        keyword.type = "text";
        keyword.placeholder = "예: 자동화, BIM, 공사일보, 부동산 정책";

        count.id = "ai-count";
        count.name = "count";
        count.type = "hidden";

        selected.id = "ai-selected-keywords";
        selected.name = "selected_keywords";
        selected.type = "hidden";

        let panel = document.getElementById("cblForceTopRowPanel");

        if (!panel) {
            panel = document.createElement("div");
            panel.id = "cblForceTopRowPanel";
            panel.className = "cbl-force-top-row-panel";
            panel.innerHTML = `
                <div class="cbl-force-top-row-head">
                    <div>자동글 카테고리</div>
                    <div>본문 이미지 개수</div>
                    <div>키워드 입력</div>
                    <div>삭제</div>
                    <div>추가</div>
                </div>
                <div class="cbl-force-top-row"></div>
            `;
        }

        const row = panel.querySelector(".cbl-force-top-row");

        row.innerHTML = "";
        row.appendChild(category);
        row.appendChild(image);
        row.appendChild(keyword);

        const del = document.createElement("button");
        del.type = "button";
        del.className = "cbl-force-del";
        del.textContent = "삭제";

        const add = document.createElement("button");
        add.type = "button";
        add.className = "cbl-force-add";
        add.textContent = "추가";

        row.appendChild(del);
        row.appendChild(add);

        panel.appendChild(count);
        panel.appendChild(selected);

        const writingGroup = findWritingGroup(form);
        if (writingGroup) {
            writingGroup.insertAdjacentElement("beforebegin", panel);
        } else {
            const csrf = form.querySelector('input[name="csrfmiddlewaretoken"]');
            if (csrf) {
                csrf.insertAdjacentElement("afterend", panel);
            } else {
                form.prepend(panel);
            }
        }

        const extraGroup = findExtraGroup(form);

        let todayBox = document.getElementById("cblTodayKeywordBox");

        if (!todayBox) {
            todayBox = document.createElement("div");
            todayBox.id = "cblTodayKeywordBox";
        }

        let btn = document.getElementById("aiKeywordRecommendBtn");
        let recoBox = document.getElementById("aiKeywordRecommendBox");

        if (!btn) {
            btn = document.createElement("button");
            btn.type = "button";
            btn.id = "aiKeywordRecommendBtn";
            btn.className = "ai-keyword-recommend-btn";
            btn.textContent = "오늘자 키워드 추천";
        }

        if (!recoBox) {
            recoBox = document.createElement("div");
            recoBox.id = "aiKeywordRecommendBox";
            recoBox.className = "ai-keyword-recommend-box";
            recoBox.hidden = true;
            recoBox.innerHTML = `
                <div class="ai-keyword-recommend-head">
                    <div class="ai-keyword-head-left">
                        <span class="ai-keyword-mini-label">TREND PICK</span>
                        <strong>추천 키워드 리스트</strong>
                        <p>추천 키워드를 누르면 위 키워드 입력칸에 자동으로 추가됩니다.</p>
                    </div>
                    <div class="ai-keyword-count-badge">10개 추천</div>
                </div>
                <div id="aiKeywordRecommendList" class="ai-keyword-recommend-list"></div>
            `;
        }

        if (!todayBox.contains(btn)) todayBox.appendChild(btn);
        if (!todayBox.contains(recoBox)) todayBox.appendChild(recoBox);

        if (extraGroup) {
            extraGroup.insertAdjacentElement("afterend", todayBox);
        }

        hideEmptyOldBlocks(form, panel, todayBox);

        del.onclick = function () {
            keyword.value = "";
            selected.value = "";
            count.value = "1";
            keyword.focus();
        };

        add.onclick = function () {
            keyword.focus();
        };

        function syncCount() {
            const arr = String(keyword.value || "")
                .split(",")
                .map(function (v) { return v.trim(); })
                .filter(Boolean);

            count.value = String(Math.max(arr.length, 1));
            selected.value = arr.length ? JSON.stringify(arr) : "";
        }

        keyword.addEventListener("input", syncCount);
        form.addEventListener("submit", syncCount);
        syncCount();
    }

    ready(function () {
        mountTopRow();
        setTimeout(mountTopRow, 200);
        setTimeout(mountTopRow, 700);
        setTimeout(mountTopRow, 1500);
    });
})();
</script>
<!-- CBL_HARD_RENDER_TOP_KEYWORD_ROW_END -->

<script>
(function () {
    if (window.__CBL_TODAY_KEYWORD_FULL_FIX__) return;
    window.__CBL_TODAY_KEYWORD_FULL_FIX__ = true;

    const CATEGORIES = [
        { value: "architecture", label: "건축" },
        { value: "realestate", label: "부동산" },
        { value: "finance", label: "금융" },
        { value: "tech", label: "테크" },
        { value: "life", label: "일상" }
    ];

    const CATEGORY_TO_VALUE = {
        "건축": "architecture",
        "부동산": "realestate",
        "금융": "finance",
        "테크": "tech",
        "일상": "life",
        "architecture": "architecture",
        "realestate": "realestate",
        "finance": "finance",
        "tech": "tech",
        "life": "life"
    };

    const CATEGORY_TO_LABEL = {
        "architecture": "건축",
        "realestate": "부동산",
        "finance": "금융",
        "tech": "테크",
        "life": "일상"
    };

    function clean(value) {
        return String(value || "").replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim();
    }

    function csrfToken() {
        const tokenInput = document.querySelector("#aiPostForm input[name='csrfmiddlewaretoken']");
        return tokenInput ? tokenInput.value : "";
    }

    function getPanel() {
        return document.getElementById("cblForceTopRowPanel");
    }

    function ensureBody() {
        const panel = getPanel();
        if (!panel) return null;

        let body = panel.querySelector(".cbl-force-top-row-body");

        if (!body) {
            body = document.createElement("div");
            body.className = "cbl-force-top-row-body";

            const firstRow = panel.querySelector(".cbl-force-top-row");
            const hiddenStart = panel.querySelector("input[type='hidden']");

            if (firstRow) {
                firstRow.insertAdjacentElement("beforebegin", body);
                body.appendChild(firstRow);
            } else if (hiddenStart) {
                hiddenStart.insertAdjacentElement("beforebegin", body);
            } else {
                panel.appendChild(body);
            }
        }

        return body;
    }

    function categoryOptions(selected) {
        selected = CATEGORY_TO_VALUE[selected] || selected || "architecture";

        return CATEGORIES.map(function (item) {
            return `<option value="${item.value}" ${item.value === selected ? "selected" : ""}>${item.label}</option>`;
        }).join("");
    }

    function imageOptions(selected) {
        selected = String(selected || "0");

        return ["0", "1", "2", "3", "4", "5"].map(function (value) {
            return `<option value="${value}" ${value === selected ? "selected" : ""}>${value}장</option>`;
        }).join("");
    }

    function escapeHtml(value) {
        return clean(value)
            .replaceAll("&", "&amp;")
            .replaceAll("<", "&lt;")
            .replaceAll(">", "&gt;")
            .replaceAll('"', "&quot;")
            .replaceAll("'", "&#039;");
    }

    function rowHtml(data) {
        data = data || {};

        return `
            <div class="cbl-force-top-row" data-keyword="${escapeHtml(data.keyword || "")}">
                <select class="cbl-row-category">${categoryOptions(data.category || "architecture")}</select>
                <select class="cbl-row-image-count">${imageOptions(data.image_count || "0")}</select>
                <input class="cbl-row-keyword" type="text" value="${escapeHtml(data.keyword || "")}" placeholder="예: 자동화, BIM, 공사일보, 부동산 정책">
                <button type="button" class="cbl-force-del">삭제</button>
                <button type="button" class="cbl-force-add">추가</button>
            </div>
        `;
    }

    function rows() {
        const body = ensureBody();
        return body ? Array.from(body.querySelectorAll(".cbl-force-top-row")) : [];
    }

    function rowData() {
        return rows().map(function (row) {
            const category = row.querySelector(".cbl-row-category");
            const image = row.querySelector(".cbl-row-image-count");
            const keyword = row.querySelector(".cbl-row-keyword");

            return {
                category: category ? category.value : "architecture",
                image_count: image ? image.value : "0",
                keyword: keyword ? clean(keyword.value) : ""
            };
        }).filter(function (item) {
            return item.keyword;
        });
    }

    function currentImageCount() {
        const first = rows()[0];
        const image = first ? first.querySelector(".cbl-row-image-count") : null;
        return image ? image.value : "0";
    }

    function renderRows(items) {
        const body = ensureBody();
        if (!body) return;

        if (!items.length) {
            items = [{ category: "architecture", image_count: "0", keyword: "" }];
        }

        body.innerHTML = items.map(rowHtml).join("");
        syncForm();
    }

    function addKeyword(data) {
        const items = rowData();

        if (items.some(function (item) { return item.keyword === data.keyword; })) {
            renderRows(items.filter(function (item) { return item.keyword !== data.keyword; }));
            return;
        }

        items.push(data);
        renderRows(items);
    }

    function syncForm() {
        const form = document.getElementById("aiPostForm");
        if (!form) return;

        const allRows = rows();
        const items = rowData();
        const keywords = items.map(function (item) { return item.keyword; });

        allRows.forEach(function (row, index) {
            const category = row.querySelector(".cbl-row-category");
            const image = row.querySelector(".cbl-row-image-count");
            const keyword = row.querySelector(".cbl-row-keyword");

            [category, image, keyword].forEach(function (el) {
                if (!el) return;
                el.removeAttribute("id");
                el.removeAttribute("name");
            });

            if (index === 0) {
                if (category) {
                    category.id = "ai-category";
                    category.name = "category";
                }

                if (image) {
                    image.id = "ai-image-count";
                    image.name = "image_count";
                }

                if (keyword) {
                    keyword.id = "ai-keywords";
                    keyword.name = "keywords";
                }
            }
        });

        let countInput = document.getElementById("ai-count");
        if (!countInput) {
            countInput = document.createElement("input");
            countInput.type = "hidden";
            countInput.id = "ai-count";
            countInput.name = "count";
            form.appendChild(countInput);
        }

        let selectedInput = document.getElementById("ai-selected-keywords");
        if (!selectedInput) {
            selectedInput = document.createElement("input");
            selectedInput.type = "hidden";
            selectedInput.id = "ai-selected-keywords";
            selectedInput.name = "selected_keywords";
            form.appendChild(selectedInput);
        }

        countInput.value = Math.max(keywords.length, 1);
        selectedInput.value = keywords.length ? JSON.stringify(keywords) : "";

        document.querySelectorAll("#aiKeywordRecommendList .ai-keyword-chip").forEach(function (chip) {
            const keyword = clean(chip.dataset.keyword);

            if (keyword && keywords.includes(keyword)) {
                chip.classList.add("selected");

                const txt = chip.querySelector(".ai-keyword-select-text");
                if (txt) txt.textContent = "선택됨";
            } else {
                chip.classList.remove("selected");

                const txt = chip.querySelector(".ai-keyword-select-text");
                if (txt) txt.textContent = "선택";
            }
        });
    }

    function normalizeKeywords(data, fallbackCategory) {
        const raw = Array.isArray(data.keywords) ? data.keywords : [];

        return raw.map(function (item) {
            if (typeof item === "string") {
                return {
                    category: fallbackCategory.label,
                    categoryValue: fallbackCategory.value,
                    keyword: clean(item),
                    reason: ""
                };
            }

            return {
                category: fallbackCategory.label,
                categoryValue: fallbackCategory.value,
                keyword: clean(item.keyword || item.title || item.text || ""),
                reason: clean(item.reason || item.description || "")
            };
        }).filter(function (item) {
            return item.keyword;
        }).slice(0, 5);
    }

    function renderRecommendList(items) {
        const list = document.getElementById("aiKeywordRecommendList");
        const badge = document.querySelector("#aiKeywordRecommendBox .ai-keyword-count-badge");

        if (!list) return;

        list.innerHTML = "";

        if (badge) {
            badge.textContent = `${items.length}개 추천`;
        }

        items.forEach(function (item) {
            const categoryValue = CATEGORY_TO_VALUE[item.categoryValue] || item.categoryValue || "architecture";
            const btn = document.createElement("button");

            btn.type = "button";
            btn.className = "ai-keyword-chip";
            btn.dataset.keyword = item.keyword;
            btn.dataset.category = categoryValue;

            btn.innerHTML = `
                <div class="ai-keyword-chip-top">
                    <span class="ai-keyword-category">${escapeHtml(CATEGORY_TO_LABEL[categoryValue] || item.category)}</span>
                    <span class="ai-keyword-select-text">선택</span>
                </div>
                <strong>${escapeHtml(item.keyword)}</strong>
                <p>${escapeHtml(item.reason || "추천 키워드")}</p>
            `;

            list.appendChild(btn);
        });

        syncForm();
    }

    async function fetchCategoryKeywords(category) {
        const fd = new FormData();
        fd.append("category", category.value);

        const res = await fetch("{% url 'ai_keyword_recommend' %}", {
            method: "POST",
            headers: {
                "X-CSRFToken": csrfToken()
            },
            body: fd
        });

        const data = await res.json();

        if (!data.ok) {
            throw new Error(data.message || `${category.label} 키워드 추천 실패`);
        }

        return normalizeKeywords(data, category);
    }

    async function loadTodayKeywords() {
        const box = document.getElementById("aiKeywordRecommendBox");
        const list = document.getElementById("aiKeywordRecommendList");
        const btn = document.getElementById("aiKeywordRecommendBtn");

        if (!box || !list || !btn) return;

        box.hidden = false;
        list.innerHTML = `
            <div style="grid-column:1/-1;padding:18px;border-radius:18px;background:#f8fafc;font-weight:900;color:#334155;">
                건축 · 부동산 · 금융 · 테크 · 일상 키워드를 5개씩 불러오는 중...
            </div>
        `;

        const originalText = btn.textContent;
        btn.disabled = true;
        btn.textContent = "추천 중...";

        try {
            const results = await Promise.allSettled(
                CATEGORIES.map(function (category) {
                    return fetchCategoryKeywords(category);
                })
            );

            const items = results.flatMap(function (result) {
                return result.status === "fulfilled" ? result.value : [];
            });

            if (!items.length) {
                throw new Error("추천 키워드를 불러오지 못했습니다.");
            }

            renderRecommendList(items);
        } catch (err) {
            list.innerHTML = `
                <div style="grid-column:1/-1;padding:18px;border-radius:18px;background:#fee2e2;color:#991b1b;font-weight:900;">
                    ${escapeHtml(err.message || "키워드 추천에 실패했습니다.")}
                </div>
            `;
        } finally {
            btn.disabled = false;
            btn.textContent = originalText || "✨ 오늘자 키워드 추천";
        }
    }

    document.addEventListener("click", function (event) {
        const recommendBtn = event.target.closest("#aiKeywordRecommendBtn");

        if (recommendBtn) {
            event.preventDefault();
            event.stopPropagation();
            event.stopImmediatePropagation();
            loadTodayKeywords();
            return;
        }

        const chip = event.target.closest("#aiKeywordRecommendList .ai-keyword-chip");

        if (chip) {
            event.preventDefault();
            event.stopPropagation();
            event.stopImmediatePropagation();

            addKeyword({
                category: chip.dataset.category || "architecture",
                image_count: currentImageCount(),
                keyword: clean(chip.dataset.keyword)
            });

            return;
        }

        const addBtn = event.target.closest("#cblForceTopRowPanel .cbl-force-add");

        if (addBtn) {
            event.preventDefault();

            /*
             * 추가 버튼에서는 빈 키워드 행도 보존해야 한다.
             * 기존 rowData()는 빈 키워드를 제거하므로 직접 전체 행을 읽는다.
             */
            const items = rows().map(function (row) {
                const category = row.querySelector(".cbl-row-category");
                const image = row.querySelector(".cbl-row-image-count");
                const keyword = row.querySelector(".cbl-row-keyword");

                return {
                    category: category ? category.value : "architecture",
                    image_count: image ? image.value : "0",
                    keyword: keyword ? clean(keyword.value) : ""
                };
            });

            const sourceRow = addBtn.closest(".cbl-force-top-row");
            const sourceCategory = sourceRow
                ? sourceRow.querySelector(".cbl-row-category")
                : null;
            const sourceImage = sourceRow
                ? sourceRow.querySelector(".cbl-row-image-count")
                : null;

            items.push({
                category: sourceCategory ? sourceCategory.value : "architecture",
                image_count: sourceImage ? sourceImage.value : currentImageCount(),
                keyword: ""
            });

            renderRows(items);

            const updatedRows = rows();
            const newRow = updatedRows[updatedRows.length - 1];
            const newInput = newRow
                ? newRow.querySelector(".cbl-row-keyword")
                : null;

            if (newInput) {
                newInput.focus();
            }

            return;
        }

        const delBtn = event.target.closest("#cblForceTopRowPanel .cbl-force-del");

        if (delBtn) {
            event.preventDefault();

            const row = delBtn.closest(".cbl-force-top-row");
            if (!row) return;

            const allRows = rows();

            if (allRows.length <= 1) {
                const input = row.querySelector(".cbl-row-keyword");
                if (input) input.value = "";
            } else {
                row.remove();
            }

            syncForm();
        }
    }, true);

    document.addEventListener("input", function (event) {
        if (event.target.closest("#cblForceTopRowPanel")) {
            syncForm();
        }
    }, true);

    document.addEventListener("change", function (event) {
        if (event.target.closest("#cblForceTopRowPanel")) {
            syncForm();
        }
    }, true);

    document.addEventListener("submit", function (event) {
        if (event.target && event.target.id === "aiPostForm") {
            syncForm();
        }
    }, true);

    function boot() {
        ensureBody();
        syncForm();
    }

    if (document.readyState === "loading") {
        document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function () {
            setTimeout(boot, 50);
            setTimeout(boot, 400);
            setTimeout(boot, 1000);
        });
    } else {
        setTimeout(boot, 50);
        setTimeout(boot, 400);
        setTimeout(boot, 1000);
    }
})();
</script>
<!-- CBL_TODAY_KEYWORD_FULL_FIX_END -->


<!-- CBL_AUTO_KEYWORD_CATEGORY_LOCK_START -->
<script>
(function(){
    const CBL_CATEGORY_MAP = {
        "건축": "architecture",
        "건설": "architecture",
        "architecture": "architecture",

        "부동산": "realestate",
        "realestate": "realestate",
        "real_estate": "realestate",

        "금융": "finance",
        "경제": "finance",
        "finance": "finance",

        "테크": "tech",
        "기술": "tech",
        "IT": "tech",
        "it": "tech",
        "tech": "tech",

        "일상": "life",
        "라이프": "life",
        "life": "life"
    };

    const CBL_VALID_CATEGORIES = new Set(["architecture", "realestate", "finance", "tech", "life"]);

    function cblNorm(v){
        return String(v || "").replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim();
    }

    function cblCategorySlug(v){
        const key = cblNorm(v);
        const slug = CBL_CATEGORY_MAP[key] || key;
        return CBL_VALID_CATEGORIES.has(slug) ? slug : "";
    }

    function cblIsImageCount(v){
        const s = cblNorm(v);
        return /^-?\d+\s*(장)?$/.test(s);
    }

    function cblIsCategoryValue(v){
        return !!cblCategorySlug(v);
    }

    function cblClosestRow(el){
        return (
            el.closest("[data-cbl-auto-row]") ||
            el.closest(".cbl-auto-keyword-row") ||
            el.closest(".cbl-ai-auto-keyword-row") ||
            el.closest(".cbl-keyword-row") ||
            el.closest(".keyword-row") ||
            el.closest(".auto-keyword-row") ||
            el.closest("tr") ||
            el.parentElement
        );
    }

    function cblCollectKeywordCategoryMap(){
        const map = new Map();

        const inputs = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll("input, select, textarea"));

        const keywordInputs = inputs.filter(el => {
            const name = cblNorm(el.getAttribute("name")).toLowerCase();
            const placeholder = cblNorm(el.getAttribute("placeholder"));
            const value = cblNorm(el.value);

            if (!value) return false;
            if (cblIsCategoryValue(value)) return false;
            if (cblIsImageCount(value)) return false;
            if (el.type === "hidden") return false;
            if (el.type === "button" || el.type === "submit") return false;

            return (
                name.includes("keyword") ||
                placeholder.includes("키워드") ||
                value.length >= 3
            );
        });

        keywordInputs.forEach(keywordEl => {
            const keyword = cblNorm(keywordEl.value);
            if (!keyword || cblIsCategoryValue(keyword) || cblIsImageCount(keyword)) return;

            const row = cblClosestRow(keywordEl);
            if (!row) return;

            const rowFields = Array.from(row.querySelectorAll("input, select, textarea"));
            let category = "";

            for (const field of rowFields) {
                if (field === keywordEl) continue;

                const value = cblNorm(field.value);
                const slug = cblCategorySlug(value);
                if (slug) {
                    category = slug;
                    break;
                }

                const dataCategory = cblCategorySlug(field.dataset.category || field.dataset.slug || "");
                if (dataCategory) {
                    category = dataCategory;
                    break;
                }
            }

            const rowDataCategory = cblCategorySlug(row.dataset.category || row.dataset.slug || "");
            if (!category && rowDataCategory) category = rowDataCategory;

            if (category) {
                map.set(keyword, category);
            }
        });

        return map;
    }

    function cblFindCategoryForFormData(fd){
        const map = cblCollectKeywordCategoryMap();
        if (!map.size) return "";

        const keys = [
            "keyword",
            "keywords",
            "keyword_input",
            "title",
            "post_title",
            "auto_keywords[]",
            "auto_keywords",
            "keywords[]"
        ];

        for (const key of keys) {
            const values = fd.getAll(key);
            for (const value of values) {
                const keyword = cblNorm(value);
                if (map.has(keyword)) return map.get(keyword);
            }
        }

        for (const value of fd.values()) {
            const keyword = cblNorm(value);
            if (map.has(keyword)) return map.get(keyword);
        }

        return "";
    }

    function cblForceCategoryOnFormData(fd){
        if (!(fd instanceof FormData)) return fd;

        const forced = cblFindCategoryForFormData(fd);
        if (!forced) return fd;

        [
            "category",
            "post_category",
            "post_category_slug",
            "selected_category",
            "ai_category",
            "auto_category",
            "cbl_force_category"
        ].forEach(name => {
            try { fd.set(name, forced); } catch(e) {}
        });

        return fd;
    }

    function cblSyncVisibleRowsIntoHiddenInputs(form){
        if (!form) return;

        form.querySelectorAll(".cbl-category-lock-hidden").forEach(el => el.remove());

        const map = cblCollectKeywordCategoryMap();

        for (const [keyword, category] of map.entries()) {
            const hCat = document.createElement("input");
            hCat.type = "hidden";
            hCat.name = "auto_categories[]";
            hCat.value = category;
            hCat.className = "cbl-category-lock-hidden";

            const hKey = document.createElement("input");
            hKey.type = "hidden";
            hKey.name = "auto_keywords[]";
            hKey.value = keyword;
            hKey.className = "cbl-category-lock-hidden";

            form.appendChild(hCat);
            form.appendChild(hKey);
        }
    }

    document.addEventListener("submit", function(e){
        const form = e.target;
        if (!(form instanceof HTMLFormElement)) return;

        const action = String(form.getAttribute("action") || "");
        const isAiGenerate = action.includes("/ai-post/generate") || location.pathname.includes("/ai-post");

        cblSyncVisibleRowsIntoHiddenInputs(form);

        if (isAiGenerate) {
            const fd = new FormData(form);
            const forced = cblFindCategoryForFormData(fd);
            if (forced) {
                let hidden = form.querySelector('input[name="cbl_force_category"]');
                if (!hidden) {
                    hidden = document.createElement("input");
                    hidden.type = "hidden";
                    hidden.name = "cbl_force_category";
                    hidden.className = "cbl-category-lock-hidden";
                    form.appendChild(hidden);
                }
                hidden.value = forced;
            }
        }
    }, true);

    if (window.fetch) {
        const originalFetch = window.fetch;
        window.fetch = function(input, init){
            try {
                if (init && init.body instanceof FormData) {
                    init.body = cblForceCategoryOnFormData(init.body);
                }
            } catch(e) {}
            return originalFetch.apply(this, arguments);
        };
    }

    if (window.XMLHttpRequest) {
        const originalOpen = XMLHttpRequest.prototype.open;
        const originalSend = XMLHttpRequest.prototype.send;

        XMLHttpRequest.prototype.open = function(method, url){
            this._cblUrl = String(url || "");
            return originalOpen.apply(this, arguments);
        };

        XMLHttpRequest.prototype.send = function(body){
            try {
                if (body instanceof FormData) {
                    body = cblForceCategoryOnFormData(body);
                }
            } catch(e) {}
            return originalSend.call(this, body);
        };
    }

    window.cblCollectKeywordCategoryMap = cblCollectKeywordCategoryMap;
})();
</script>
<!-- CBL_AUTO_KEYWORD_CATEGORY_LOCK_END -->


<!-- CBL_AI_ROW_PAYLOAD_LOCK_START -->
<script>
(function () {
    if (window.__CBL_AI_ROW_PAYLOAD_LOCK__) return;
    window.__CBL_AI_ROW_PAYLOAD_LOCK__ = true;

    const CATEGORY_MAP = {
        "건축": "architecture",
        "건설": "architecture",
        "architecture": "architecture",

        "부동산": "realestate",
        "realestate": "realestate",
        "real_estate": "realestate",

        "금융": "finance",
        "경제": "finance",
        "finance": "finance",

        "테크": "tech",
        "기술": "tech",
        "IT": "tech",
        "it": "tech",
        "tech": "tech",

        "일상": "life",
        "라이프": "life",
        "life": "life"
    };

    const VALID = new Set(["architecture", "realestate", "finance", "tech", "life"]);

    function clean(v) {
        return String(v || "").replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim();
    }

    function normCategory(v) {
        const s = clean(v);
        const slug = CATEGORY_MAP[s] || s;
        return VALID.has(slug) ? slug : "tech";
    }

    function collectRows(form) {
        const rows = Array.from(form.querySelectorAll(".cbl-force-top-row"));
        const items = [];

        rows.forEach(function (row) {
            const categoryEl = row.querySelector(".cbl-row-category");
            const imageEl = row.querySelector(".cbl-row-image-count");
            const keywordEl = row.querySelector(".cbl-row-keyword");

            const keyword = clean(keywordEl ? keywordEl.value : "");

            if (!keyword) return;

            items.push({
                category: normCategory(categoryEl ? categoryEl.value : ""),
                image_count: clean(imageEl ? imageEl.value : "0").replace("장", "") || "0",
                keyword: keyword
            });
        });

        // 구형 구조 fallback
        if (!items.length) {
            const categoryEl = form.querySelector("#ai-category, select[name='category']");
            const imageEl = form.querySelector("#ai-image-count, select[name='image_count']");
            const keywordEl = form.querySelector("#ai-keywords, input[name='keywords']");

            const raw = clean(keywordEl ? keywordEl.value : "");
            const parts = raw
                ? raw.split(",").map(function (v) { return clean(v); }).filter(Boolean)
                : [];

            parts.forEach(function (keyword) {
                items.push({
                    category: normCategory(categoryEl ? categoryEl.value : ""),
                    image_count: clean(imageEl ? imageEl.value : "0").replace("장", "") || "0",
                    keyword: keyword
                });
            });
        }

        return items;
    }

    function setHidden(form, name, value) {
        let el = form.querySelector('input[name="' + name + '"]');

        if (!el) {
            el = document.createElement("input");
            el.type = "hidden";
            el.name = name;
            el.className = "cbl-ai-row-payload-hidden";
            form.appendChild(el);
        }

        el.value = value;
        return el;
    }

    function syncPayload(form) {
        if (!form || form.id !== "aiPostForm") return;

        const items = collectRows(form);

        form.querySelectorAll(".cbl-ai-row-array-hidden").forEach(function (el) {
            el.remove();
        });

        setHidden(form, "cbl_keyword_rows", JSON.stringify(items));
        setHidden(form, "selected_keywords", JSON.stringify(items.map(function (item) {
            return item.keyword;
        })));

        const countEl = form.querySelector("#ai-count, input[name='count']");
        if (countEl) {
            countEl.value = Math.max(items.length, 1);
        }

        const first = items[0];

        if (first) {
            const categoryEl = form.querySelector("#ai-category, select[name='category'], input[name='category']");
            const imageEl = form.querySelector("#ai-image-count, select[name='image_count'], input[name='image_count']");
            const keywordEl = form.querySelector("#ai-keywords, input[name='keywords']");

            if (categoryEl) categoryEl.value = first.category;
            if (imageEl) imageEl.value = first.image_count;
            if (keywordEl) keywordEl.value = first.keyword;

            setHidden(form, "category", first.category);
            setHidden(form, "image_count", first.image_count);
            setHidden(form, "keywords", first.keyword);
        }

        items.forEach(function (item) {
            const hKeyword = document.createElement("input");
            hKeyword.type = "hidden";
            hKeyword.name = "cbl_row_keywords[]";
            hKeyword.value = item.keyword;
            hKeyword.className = "cbl-ai-row-array-hidden";
            form.appendChild(hKeyword);

            const hCategory = document.createElement("input");
            hCategory.type = "hidden";
            hCategory.name = "cbl_row_categories[]";
            hCategory.value = item.category;
            hCategory.className = "cbl-ai-row-array-hidden";
            form.appendChild(hCategory);

            const hImage = document.createElement("input");
            hImage.type = "hidden";
            hImage.name = "cbl_row_image_counts[]";
            hImage.value = item.image_count;
            hImage.className = "cbl-ai-row-array-hidden";
            form.appendChild(hImage);
        });

        console.log("[CBL] AI row payload synced:", items);
    }

    document.addEventListener("input", function (e) {
        const form = document.getElementById("aiPostForm");
        if (!form) return;

        if (
            e.target &&
            (
                e.target.classList.contains("cbl-row-category") ||
                e.target.classList.contains("cbl-row-image-count") ||
                e.target.classList.contains("cbl-row-keyword")
            )
        ) {
            syncPayload(form);
        }
    }, true);

    document.addEventListener("click", function () {
        const form = document.getElementById("aiPostForm");
        if (!form) return;
        setTimeout(function () {
            syncPayload(form);
        }, 50);
    }, true);

    document.addEventListener("submit", function (e) {
        const form = e.target;
        if (!form || form.id !== "aiPostForm") return;
        syncPayload(form);
    }, true);

    document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function () {
        const form = document.getElementById("aiPostForm");
        if (!form) return;

        syncPayload(form);
        setTimeout(function () { syncPayload(form); }, 300);
        setTimeout(function () { syncPayload(form); }, 800);
    });
})();
</script>
<!-- CBL_AI_ROW_PAYLOAD_LOCK_END -->

</body>
</html>

    .thumbnail-text-overlay[class*="thumb-style-"] {
        max-width: 78% !important;
        padding: 7px 11px !important;
        font-size: 14px !important;
        border-radius: 11px !important;
    }

    .thumbnail-detail-text-overlay[class*="thumb-style-"] {
        max-width: 78% !important;
        padding: 10px 15px !important;
        font-size: clamp(19px, 6vw, 26px) !important;
        border-radius: 12px !important;
    }

    .thumbnail-text-overlay.thumb-style-light-left,
    .thumbnail-detail-text-overlay.thumb-style-light-left,
    .thumbnail-text-overlay.thumb-style-dark-bottom,
    .thumbnail-detail-text-overlay.thumb-style-dark-bottom {
        left: 5% !important;
    }

    .thumbnail-text-overlay.thumb-style-outline-right,
    .thumbnail-detail-text-overlay.thumb-style-outline-right {
        right: 5% !important;
    }
}

/* CBL_THUMBNAIL_VISUAL_VARIETY_END */

/* CBL_THUMBNAIL_CENTER_POSITION_START */

/*
 * 썸네일 박스의 색상과 모양은 기존 스타일을 유지하고,
 * 위치만 모든 화면에서 정중앙으로 통일한다.
 */
.thumbnail-text-overlay[class*="thumb-style-"],
.thumbnail-detail-text-overlay[class*="thumb-style-"] {
    left: 50% !important;
    right: auto !important;
    top: 50% !important;
    bottom: auto !important;

    transform: translate(-50%, -50%) !important;

    text-align: center !important;
    margin: 0 !important;
}

/* 모바일에서도 동일하게 중앙 고정 */
@media (max-width: 640px) {
    .thumbnail-text-overlay[class*="thumb-style-"],
    .thumbnail-detail-text-overlay[class*="thumb-style-"] {
        left: 50% !important;
        right: auto !important;
        top: 50% !important;
        bottom: auto !important;

        transform: translate(-50%, -50%) !important;
    }
}
